 <body>
  <a name="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050"></a><a name="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050"></a>
  <h1 class="topictitle1">Ascend C APIs</h1>
  <div id="body0000001917094304">
   <div class="p" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001762058545_en-us_topic_0000001589265601_p131911195110">
    <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001762058545_en-us_topic_0000001589265601_ph10546143515149">Ascend C</span> provides a group of class library APIs. You can use the standard C++ syntax and class library APIs for programming. <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001762058545_ph19399631793">Ascend C</span> programming class library APIs are classified into the following types:
    <ul id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ul178033061311">
     <li id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li16122723171312"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b955189174913">Basic data structures</strong>: basic data structures used in kernel APIs, such as <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b185510911496">GlobalTensor</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b15559919498">LocalTensor</strong>.</li>
     <li id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li16880164615139"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b36684192494">C APIs at the language extension layer</strong>: Open the complete programming capabilities of chips, support memory allocation for arrays, and generally use pointer-based programming, providing a consistent C language programming experience with the industry.</li>
     <li id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li58329249132"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b21584534497">Basic APIs</strong>: implement abstract hardware capabilities and open chip capabilities to ensure completeness and compatibility. The APIs marked as Instruction Set Architecture Special Interface (ISASI, hardware architecture-related APIs) do not guarantee compatibility across hardware versions.</li>
     <li id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li7347152641316"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b174913508">High-level APIs</strong>: implement common compute algorithms to improve programming and development efficiency based on basic APIs. High-level APIs include math library, Matmul, Softmax, and others, and ensure compatibility.</li>
     <li id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li15963594289"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b157364512321">Single Instruction Multiple Thread (SIMT) APIs</strong>: implement parallel computation by executing a single instruction across multiple threads. SIMT programming is mainly used for vector computing and is particularly well-suited for handling discrete memory access and complex control logic.</li>
     <li id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li67671127219"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b12466102119517">Utils APIs (common auxiliary functions)</strong>: provide various common utility classes, covering functions such as standard library, platform information acquisition, runtime compilation, and log output. These APIs empower you to efficiently develop operators and optimize performance.</li>
    </ul>
   </div>
   <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6410155335011"></p>
   <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p162780549507"><span><img class="eddx" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__image43427554509" src="figure/en-us_image_0000002562303741.png"></span></p>
   <div class="section" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section3119442205518">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">Basic Data Structure</h4>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table16268175715515" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 1 </b>Basic data structure list
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11268657105518">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1026810577553">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p226835735518">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row102681557105520">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12251181311560"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0006.html">LocalTensor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2025215484579"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4396131516585">LocalTensor is used to store data in the local memory of the AI Core. It supports the logical positions (<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0174.html">TPosition</a>) VECIN, VECOUT, VECCALC, A2, B1, B2, CO1, and CO2.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row026925718557">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p142691057115518"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0007.html">GlobalTensor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p625254835716"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph698815359590">Stores the global data of the global memory (external storage).</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row32691573557">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8269195705514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00143.html">Coordinate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1825015488573"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b14297203435213">Coordinate</strong> is essentially a tuple, and is used to indicate location information of a tensor in different dimensions, that is, a coordinate value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row926915745510">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p192691757185519"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00076.html">Layout</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1724954818576"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph198761643125913">The <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531541322_b931881219191">Layout&lt;Shape, Stride&gt;</strong> data structure is a basic template class that describes the memory layout of multi-dimensional tensors. It implements the mapping from the logical coordinate space to the one-dimensional memory address space based on the shape and stride information at compile time, providing fundamental support for complex tensor operations and hardware optimization.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row97101411165713">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5710711115712"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0011.html">TensorTrait</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p167101111155717"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph287618459597">The TensorTrait data structure is a basic template class that describes tensor-related information, including the tensor data type, logical position, and layout memory layout.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>
   <div class="section" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section117632211201">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">Basic APIs</h4>

      <caption>
       <b>Table 2 </b>Memory data movement APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row69936217246">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1799422162414">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p89941221202417">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p119488415242"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0101.html">DataCopy</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1188623312246">Performs data movement, including common data movement, enhanced data movement, tiled data movement, and real-time format conversion.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row29942216241">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p138851338243"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0106.html">Copy</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1488517337242">Performs the movement instruction between VECIN, VECCALC, and VECOUT, and supports the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6474134816547">mask</strong> operation and <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1256166185416">Data Block</span> interval operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">

       <b>Table 3 </b>Memory vector computation APIs

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">

       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row687313439911">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p389445174112">Computes the minimum value based on elements.</p></td>
       </tr>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1448115232"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0054.html">Adds</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p884518405416">Performs addition between a scalar and a vector element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p577119572225">Performs multiplication between a scalar and a vector element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1277082912579">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8441215238"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0056.html">Maxs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18672613113517">Compares each element of a vector with a scalar and returns the larger one.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row33851439175710">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9441515231"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0057.html">Mins</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18276162693419">Compares each element of a vector with a scalar and returns the smaller one.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row547912419575">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5445162310"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0060.html">LeakyRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p27096582222">Computes Leaky ReLU on the source operand element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11611570274">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1521314282290">Basic Arithmetic</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p570714385282"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00018.html">Subs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1616145717273"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph452415255306">Performs subtraction between a scalar and each element of a vector. The scalar can be before or after the vector. The scalar input can be a single element from a LocalTensor.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13124593270">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1779354482813"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00019.html">Divs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p191212595270"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph58341357153014">Performs division between a scalar and each element of a vector. The scalar can be before or after the vector. The scalar input can be a single element from a LocalTensor.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row64233134354">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="5" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p771519716593">Logical Computation</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24122327439"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0031.html">Not</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1694421944312">Performs bitwise Not based on elements.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row855712515365">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p512110487389"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0041.html">And</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p555732515368">Performs a bitwise AND operation based on elements.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19695152573617">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12122548183814"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0042.html">Or</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17348124922015">Performs a bitwise OR operation based on elements.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row20711575584">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p644313232"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0058.html">ShiftLeft</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1676331375210">Performs left shift on the source operand element-wise. The shift distance is determined by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b17822312551">scalarValue</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17751175445811">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9447119234"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0059.html">ShiftRight</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p97121651191211">Performs right shift on the source operand element-wise. The shift distance is determined by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b577819625519">scalarValue</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1739723113211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1239733112327">Logical Computation</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p76622335323"></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1691819570327"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00020.html">Ands</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3397531133218"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph107741166344">Performs bitwise AND between a scalar and each element of a vector. The scalar can be before or after the vector. The scalar input can be a single element from a LocalTensor.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2662163318324">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12888184123313"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00021.html">Ors</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p566393373216"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph11500142416349">Performs bitwise OR between a scalar and each element of a vector. The scalar can be before or after the vector. The scalar input can be a single element from a LocalTensor.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1413722319810">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="11" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12137102320816">Compound Computation</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1885912584229"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0063.html">Axpy</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1485935816222">Adds the product of each element in the source operand and a scalar to the corresponding element in the destination operand.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row167541318782">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3734133111451"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0074.html">CastDequant</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1924211214434">Quantizes the input and converts the precision.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15953122533617">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19122184810389"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0043.html">AddRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39535252363">Adds inputs element-wise, compares the result with 0, and selects the larger value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16141192653616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p112214815384"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0044.html">AddReluCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p514172623613">Adds inputs element-wise, compares the result with 0, selects the larger value, and converts precision based on the data types of the source and destination operand tensors.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row977016176378">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1312214873810"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0045.html">AddDeqRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1477071717372">Adds inputs element-wise, performs Deq quantization on the result, and then performs ReLU calculation on the result (compares the result with 0 and selects the larger value).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17935161713713">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1012244843817"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0046.html">SubRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149363171378">Computes the difference element-wise, compares the result with 0, and selects the larger value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row138841816370">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p51221848173810"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0047.html">SubReluCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p158818183378">Computes the difference element-wise, compares the result with 0, selects the larger value, and converts precision based on the data types of the source and destination operand tensors.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row132241218193712">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p212217485389"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0048.html">MulAddDst</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5224201818378">Multiplies <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b126749288111357">src0Local</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b154084643111357">src1Local</strong> element-wise, adds them to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b164156561211357">dstLocal</strong>, and saves the final result to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b78939128111357">dstLocal</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row117041522154616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19705182219464"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0049.html">MulCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12705182217466">Performs element-wise manipulation and converts the precision based on the data types of the source and destination operand tensors.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row193701818163720">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p91222048133815"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0050.html">FusedMulAdd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1037019189371">Multiplies <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b18914378611357">src0Local</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b36309523711357">dstLocal</strong> element-wise, adds <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b134739282111357">src1Local</strong>, and saves the result to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b111902937511357">dstLocal</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1075883983914">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19675842114214"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0051.html">MulAddRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p475993916396">Multiplies <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1431175685510">src0Local</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b13431145605512">dstLocal</strong> element-wise, adds them to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1143225612553">src1Local</strong>, compares the result with 0, selects the larger value, and saves the final result to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b18432185611550">dstLocal</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row187959172211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="5" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p147155915229">Comparison and Selection</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19980131874015"></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p157105910222"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0066.html">Compare</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1745910221">Compares the sizes of two tensors element by element. If the comparison result is true, the corresponding bit of the output result is 1. Otherwise, the bit is 0.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1314575919229">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1314535915221"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0067.html">Compare (Result Stored in a Register)</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p177959415503">Compares the sizes of two tensors element by element. If the comparison result is true, the corresponding bit of the output result is 1. Otherwise, the bit is 0. This interface can be used when the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b12650063211357">mask</strong> parameter is required. The result is stored in a register.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row31514186404">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7151171817406"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0068.html">Compares</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7227113135415">Compares the sizes of an element in a tensor with that of a scalar element by element. If the comparison result is true, the corresponding bit of the output result is 1. Otherwise, the bit is 0.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1798081810408">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p61901953124213"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0070.html">Select</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p298081819406">Selects the source operand src0 or src1 based on the bit value of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b70385474111357">selMask</strong> (mask used for selection) to obtain the destination operand dst. When the bit value of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b199567398211357">selMask</strong> is 1, src0 is selected. When the bit value of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b165283214411357">selMask</strong> is 0, src1 is selected.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1214111974012">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1719055394219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0071.html">GatherMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p182166215152">Selects elements from the source operand and writes them to the destination operand based on a gather mask (for data collection) that corresponds to either the binary of the built-in fixed mode or the binary of the user-defined input tensor values.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1095112164319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1357761654319">Precision Conversion Instructions</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p757710162439"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0073.html">Cast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5969213430">Converts precision based on the data types of the source and destination operand tensors.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1651510211434">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="11" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1757701610436">Reduction Computation</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1357731618438"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0076.html">ReduceMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1351515264314">Obtains the maximum value and its corresponding index position among the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row196635294315">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p457871613433"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0077.html">ReduceMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6853520201916">Obtains the minimum value and its corresponding index position among the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row77918244313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p35781716164313"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0078.html">ReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1546312559583">Sums up all input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row79421217439">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p65788161435"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0079.html">WholeReduceMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p193962509594">Computes the maximum value and index of all data in each repeat.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1961802720599">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p961972710592"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0080.html">WholeReduceMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0619192712592">Computes the minimum value and index of all data in each repeat.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2928152965915">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1492892975917"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0081.html">WholeReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p199283290594">Sums all data in each repeat.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row157712324319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3578151614430"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0082.html">BlockReduceMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p177391045819">Computes the maximum of all elements in each repeat.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17211133134315">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1057841619435"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0083.html">BlockReduceMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2021183144310">Computes the minimum of all elements in each repeat.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row193401338432">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1557814168438"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0084.html">BlockReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p998114445293">Sums all elements in each repeat. Source operands are added in binary tree mode.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row250213184316">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p957811615437"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0085.html">PairReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6502183184316">Sums two adjacent (odd and even) elements.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5812182116350">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p198129212351"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0086.html">RepeatReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1481215214350">Sums all data in each repeat. Compared with <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0081.html">WholeReduceSum</a>, it does not support the bitwise mask mode. You are advised to use <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0081.html">WholeReduceSum</a> with more comprehensive functions.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row109261892541">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p629093119547">Data Conversion</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p42901731145414"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0199.html">Transpose</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1429010318547">Performs transpose on data blocks of a 16 × 16 2D matrix, and conversion between [N,C,H,W] and [N,H,W,C].</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row476616718548">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p629033110540"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0200.html">TransDataTo5HD</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p162901631125410">Converts the NCHW format to the NC1HWC0 format. It can also be used for transposing a two-dimensional matrix data block.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row86261631104316">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p203231339194317">Data Padding</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p432393920436"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0088.html">Duplicate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9626431124314">Copies a variable or an immediate value multiple times and fills the result into a vector.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17641318431">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p232383912436"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0089.html">Brcb</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p911mcpsimp">Extracts eight elements from a given input tensor each time and fills them in eight data blocks (32 bytes) in the result tensor. Each element corresponds to a data block.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row173409456360">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24100384369"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0090.html">CreateVecIndex</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p234064518368">Creates the vector index with <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b59338952511357">firstValue</strong> as the start value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row161901532124317">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2323163915431">Data Scatter/Data Gather</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1232318396431"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0092.html">Gather</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p161901632134314">Gathers given input tensors by element to the result tensor based on the offset address tensor provided.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16331932164312">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1632373914312">Mask Operations</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p43241739154316"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0094.html">SetMaskCount</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9633133216430">Sets <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b69581140511357">mask</strong> to counter mode. In this mode, you do not need to perceive the number of iterations or process unaligned tail blocks. You can directly pass in the amount of data to be computed. The actual number of iterations is automatically inferred by the Vector Unit.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1678513323437">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15324339114312"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0095.html">SetMaskNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1785143218431">Sets <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6922644145118">mask</strong> to normal mode. This mode is the default mode. You can configure the number of iterations.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1393713216436">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p23247397438"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0096.html">SetVectorMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p993873224313">Sets <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b899054894710">mask</strong> during Vector computation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15756339433">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3324039144317"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0097.html">ResetMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p57533324312">Restores the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b80506185511357">mask</strong> value to the default (all 1s), indicating that all elements in each iteration participate in the Vector computation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row743516194407">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p23240390438">Quantization Settings</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10324173918436"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0099.html">SetDeqScale</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1238122924318">Sets the value of the DEQSCALE register.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table339023582010" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13390235192015">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2390103519209">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row839013512016">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2023773313396"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0016.html">GetBitCount</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1739063514204">Obtains the number of 0s or 1s in a binary number of the uint64_t type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5390935132010">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9743558403"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0017.html">CountLeadingZero</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p739014352201">Computes the number of leading 0s of a uint64_t number (number of 0s from the most significant bit to the first 1 in the binary number).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row187241145152620">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19725445132613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0018.html">Cast (float to half/int32_t)</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p772515454262">Converts the type of a scalar to a specified type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row129095914341">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15290059143412"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0019.html">CountBitsCntSameAsSignBit</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p05116051416">Computes the number of consecutive bits that are the same as the sign bit from the most significant bit in the binary number of the uint64_t type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row944019598346">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1062632319354"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0020.html">GetSFFValue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13440259173413">Obtains the location where the first 0 or 1 appears in a binary number of the uint64_t type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1566175913418">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9566659173420"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0021.html">Cast (float to bfloat16_t)</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p828219133366">Converts scalar data of the float type to scalar data of the bfloat16_t type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13704105910340">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16704195953414"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0022.html">Cast (multiple types to float)</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15293151211360">Converts scalar data of the bfloat16_t type to scalar data of the float type.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1267664316264" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 5 </b>Resource management APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15676154310267">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6676543192617">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p146761434266">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row367664312619">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p667654312618"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0108.html">TPipe</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6428345172717">Manages resources such as the global memory. It allocates and manages resources such as memory.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1867604312262">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p616062319285"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0120.html">GetTPipePtr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p26771543172611">Obtains the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b16989338155920">TPipe</strong> pointer for the Global Memory managed by the framework. After obtaining the pointer, you can perform TPipe-related operations.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1381344122816">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p681124418285"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0127.html">TBufPool</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1062105494410">Manually manages or reuses the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1088254310583">Unified Buffer</span>/<span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1535518221316">L1 Buffer</span> physical memory. It is mainly used when the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph791414174415">Unified Buffer</span>/<span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph7621654184416">L1 Buffer</span> physical memory is insufficient in multi-stage computing.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1032714367308">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p73271836193011"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0136.html">TQue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13327163673017">Performs EnQue and DeQue operations, and implements inter-task synchronization through queues.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6569103614302">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165691368309"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0146.html">TQueBind</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9569203683010">Binds the source and destination logical locations to determine the memory allocation location and insert the corresponding synchronization event, solving problems such as memory allocation, management, and synchronization.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row106614368306">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1866114362309"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0020.html">TBuf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12661203618308">Manages the memory occupied by some temporary variables used during <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph438819594207">Ascend C</span> programming. The memory occupied by these temporary variables can be managed using the TBuf data structure.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row158561936163012">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p545354010463"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0166.html">InitSpmBuffer</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16856103663012">Initializes the SPM buffer.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row324173716309">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1245314074616"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0167.html">WriteSpmBuffer</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17575330684">Copies the data that needs to be temporarily stored due to overflow into the SPM Buffer.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1515119377302">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19453154018462"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0168.html">ReadSpmBuffer</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1881419176258">Reads data from the SPM buffer back to the local data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1550403614616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4453154014462"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0172.html">GetUserWorkspace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19504103614618">Obtains the workspace pointer used by the user.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15918536184616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p54539406461"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0171.html">SetSysWorkSpace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4918163654611">Sets the pointer to the system workspace, as the system workspace is used by the framework communication mechanism during fused operator programming.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row196011374465">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2045304004615"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0170.html">GetSysWorkSpacePtr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p86018377467">Obtains the pointer to the system workspace.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table921112251162" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 6 </b>Synchronization control APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row975619311161">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8307249121617">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0308184961620">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1021262515169">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p62931833203117"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0178.html">TQueSync</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p82931433203116">Provides synchronization control. You can use these types of APIs to implement synchronization control.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1821272513168">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1190314213216"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0202.html">IBSet</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15692228145311">When different AI Cores operate the same global memory block, this function can be called to synchronize the AI Cores to avoid data dependency problems such as write-after-read, read-after-write, and write-after-write. <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6271173385711">IBSet</strong> is called to set the flag bit of a core. <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b182725331575">IBSet</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b0272123315711">IBWait</strong> are used in pairs to indicate the synchronization waiting instruction between cores, waiting for the completion of a core operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row121219258161">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13903164214324"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0203.html">IBWait</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p27141049175319">When different AI Cores operate the same global memory block, this function can be called to synchronize the AI Cores to avoid data dependency problems such as write-after-read, read-after-write, and write-after-write. <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b12166818195819">IBWait</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1316651835813">IBSet</strong> are used in pairs to indicate the synchronization waiting instruction between cores, waiting for the completion of a core operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1121220254168">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8904942143216"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0204.html">SyncAll</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12782512105416">When different AI Cores operate the same global memory block, this function can be called to synchronize the AI Cores to avoid data dependency problems such as write-after-read, read-after-write, and write-after-write. Currently, multi-core synchronization is classified into hardware synchronization and software synchronization. Hardware synchronization uses hardware-provided all-core synchronization instructions to ensure multi-core synchronization. Software synchronization is implemented through software algorithms.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row92121225191611">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29094917345"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0206.html">InitDetermineComputeWorkspace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9909129113414">Initializes the value of the GM shared memory. <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0207.html">WaitPreBlock</a> and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0208.html">NotifyNextBlock</a> can be called only after the initialization is complete.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16213425171615">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p390910913341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0207.html">WaitPreBlock</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p49091792347">Reads the value in the GM address to determine whether to continue to wait. When the GM value meets the waiting condition of the current core, the core can proceed to the next operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row152131825131610">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29091594341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0208.html">NotifyNextBlock</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6909899349">Writes the GM address to notify the next core that the operation of the current core is completed and the next core can perform the operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row52131925101618">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39841259102515"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00087.html">SetNextTaskStart</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12934317236"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1882043515233">Is called in the subkernels of SuperKernel. After the call, the instructions can be executed in parallel with subsequent subkernels, improving the overall performance. </span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row162135257162">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p288919718266"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00088.html">WaitPreTaskEnd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p632496172313"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1925517378244">Is called in the subkernels of SuperKernel. Before the call, the instructions can be executed in parallel with earlier subkernels, improving the overall performance. </span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table5254131810573" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 7 </b>Cache processing APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row32541018135711">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.27%" id="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17682103125810">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.730000000000004%" id="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p26828319585">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1325501835712">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.27%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p83441336473"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0176.html">DataCachePreload</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.730000000000004%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18111100154719">Preloads data from the specific DDR address where the source address is located to the data cache.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5255181815720">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.27%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1134473164714"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0177.html">DataCacheCleanAndInvalid</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.730000000000004%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p647301034415">Refreshes the cache to ensure cache consistency.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table26716458301" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 8 </b>System variable access API
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15672134518304">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18672144511306">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p56726451306">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row967212454304">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1194732015475"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0184.html">GetBlockNum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p585381495015">Obtains the number of blocks configured for the current task, which is used for multi-core logic control in the code.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1967224513011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11947620104710"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0185.html">GetBlockIdx</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p54921825165017">Obtains the index of the current core, which is used for multi-core logic control and multi-core offset computation in the code.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11672104593014">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17947182012472"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0186.html">GetDataBlockSizeInBytes</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14250113816503">Obtains the size (in bytes) of a data block of the current chip version. You can compute the values of parameters, such as <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b61361647185815">repeatTime</strong>, <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b31362047115816"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph21362047195815">Data Block Stride</span></strong>, and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b11378472588"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph141377473586">Repeat Stride</span></strong>, to be passed in the API command based on the data block size.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3672445153011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1594715207476"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0187.html">GetArchVersion</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p764334516501">Obtains the version number of the current AI processor architecture.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4284193416298">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12284163417298"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00094.html">InitSocState</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1828453452913">Initializes the AI Core state. The AI Core has some global states, such as the atomic accumulation state and mask mode. During actual running, these values can be modified by previously executed operators, resulting in unexpected computation behavior. In <a href="/document/detail/en/CANNCommunityEdition/900/programug/Ascendcopdevg/atlas_ascendc_10_00019.html">static tensor programming</a> scenarios, you must call this API at the kernel entry point.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1395854383210" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 9 </b>Atomic operation APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12958043173211">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" id="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2958104363212">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" id="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0958174353211">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1077235835018">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10729145920507"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0210.html">SetAtomicAdd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20143125311516">Sets whether to perform atomic addition for data transfer from VECOUT to GM, from L0C to GM, or from L1 to GM. The addition data type can be set based on different parameters.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row139588436327">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1772985912505"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0211.html">SetAtomicType</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p38701310125517">Sets different atomic operation data types using template parameters.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row595874313218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1870510588519"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0212.html">DisableDmaAtomic</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1214271020615">Clears the status of an atomic operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2084965011239">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1359015148246"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00259.html">AtomicAdd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p108491650102316"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1305389278">Performs atomic addition on the specified global memory address.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row171030533239">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p324502616243"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00260.html">AtomicMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18103053142317"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph169593419276">Performs atomic minimization on the specified global memory address.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1836819554234">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p142593217246"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00261.html">AtomicMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17368655132317"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph192894512276">Performs atomic maximization on the specified global memory address.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row437810019249">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p143501939152411"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00262.html">AtomicCas</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1637810162415"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1570715132720">Performs an atomic comparison on the specified global memory address. If the current value at that address equals <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562301423_b099016734613">value1</strong>, it is replaced with <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562301423_b186415213460">value2</strong>. Otherwise, the value at the global memory address remains unchanged.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1223315282416">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11984124519248"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00263.html">AtomicExch</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123311213245"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph8490145722711">Performs an atomic exchange operation in the global memory. Specifically, it reads data from the specified global memory address, stores the new value back to the same address, and returns the old value.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table18295429109" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 10 </b>Debugging APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row530154201012">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" id="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p113014281016">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" id="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1230154221011">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4220222125515">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p741723545516"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0192.html">DumpTensor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1541723514559">Dumps the content of specified tensors for operators developed based on operator projects.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row153018215552">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p144177356555"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0193.html">printf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1941711350553">Implements the formatted output function in CPU- or NPU-side debugging for operators developed based on operator projects.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18839203119378">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17828375379"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00168.html">ascendc_assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p138203733710"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph5884122310382">Provides an API for implementing the assert function in both the CPU domain and NPU domain. When the assert condition is not met, the system outputs the assert information and prints it in a formatted manner on the screen.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8256721175511">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p34186352551"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0194.html">assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1141883535519">Implements the assert function in CPU/NPU for operators developed based on operator projects.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row820972155519">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
