         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0418143555511"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0195.html">DumpAccChkPoint</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9418735175516">Dumps the content of specified tensors for operators developed based on operator projects. This API can be used to print tensors at a specified offset position.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15977214153717">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1068951853720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00002.html">PrintTimeStamp</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149786149371"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph18761185413715">Provides the timestamp logging function to mark key execution points in the operator kernel code.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14208102016559">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12418123512551"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0196.html">Trap</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1941853512557">Stops the kernel when a software exception occurs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row830164241016">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13406234125916"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1207.html">GmAlloc</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001963639306_en-us_topic_0000001541764188_p1531015912615">Creates shared memory during verification of the CPU-side operation of the kernel function. That is, creates a shared file in the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b168010534611357">/tmp</strong> directory and returns the mapping pointer to the file.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row88415865818">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p161386446595"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1208.html">ICPU_RUN_KF</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6851188585">Functions as the CPU commissioning entry and completes calls to CPU operator programs during verification of the CPU-side operation of the kernel function.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7702750145910">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19702850185918"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1209.html">ICPU_SET_TILING_KEY</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10702750175919">Specifies tilingKey used for the current CPU debugging. During debugging, only the branch to which tilingKey corresponds in the operator kernel function is executed.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10114145135910">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p31141451185911"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1210.html">GmFree</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p311495195915">Frees the shared memory allocated by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1510673115010">GmAlloc</strong> during verification of the CPU-side operation of the kernel function.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1125615511592">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16416351306"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1211.html">SetKernelMode</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001963639310_en-us_topic_0000001656094169_p1118165416116">Sets the kernel mode to the single AIV mode, single AIC mode, or MIX mode to enable CPU commissioning of single AIV (vector) operators, single AIC (cube) operators, or MIX operators, respectively.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5399851115914">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18391515913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1212.html">TRACE_START</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p133991451205919">Performs dotting in any running phase of the operator when the CAModel is used for operator performance simulation, to analyze the pipeline diagrams of different instructions for further performance tuning.</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3807143716119">Indicates dotting from the start point. This API is used together with <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1213.html">TRACE_STOP</a>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row125261851195913">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p66051550125"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1213.html">TRACE_STOP</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002000199857_p15636113710169">Performs dotting in any running phase of the operator when the CAModel is used for operator performance simulation, to analyze the pipeline diagrams of different instructions for further performance tuning.</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002000199857_p868234414164">Indicates dotting from the end point. This API is used together with <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1212.html">TRACE_START</a>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row368519581124">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1468611581226"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1214.html">MetricsProfStart</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16869582024">Starts the profile data collection. This API is used together with <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1953491595213">MetricsProfStop</strong>. When using <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1916349104"><a href="https://www.hiascend.com/document/detail/en/CANNCommunityEdition/900/devaids/optool/docs/en/user_guide/msopprof_user_guide.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Operator Tuning (msProf)</a></span> for on-board operator tuning, you can call <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1316349201">MetricsProfStart</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b171631891101">MetricsProfStop</strong> before and after the code segment on the kernel to specify the scope of the code segment to be optimized.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2143959125">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4507261733"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1215.html">MetricsProfStop</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002000280001_en-us_topic_0000001960477980_p279125418620">Stops the profile data collection. This API is used together with <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b9584104816524">MetricsProfStart</strong>. When using <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph01044163017"><a href="https://www.hiascend.com/document/detail/en/CANNCommunityEdition/900/devaids/optool/docs/en/user_guide/msopprof_user_guide.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Operator Tuning (msProf)</a></span> for on-board operator tuning, you can call <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b191048168016">MetricsProfStart</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b21042161409">MetricsProfStop</strong> before and after the code segment on the kernel to specify the scope of the code segment to be optimized.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table9496143191816" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 11 </b>Tool function APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14962043181812">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" id="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p449784310180">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" id="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17497204317180">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row649710437184">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149744319183"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00163.html">Async</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p816712203211"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph585624314195">Provides a unified API for executing specific functions in different modes (AIC or AIV), thereby avoiding direct hardware condition judgment in code (such as using <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381278_b34231551181">ASCEND_IS_AIV</strong> or <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381278_b34239556187">ASCEND_IS_AIC</strong>).</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2010610593211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p317115102325"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00045.html">NumericLimits</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17106857321"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1575202123315"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562301165_b92056472149">NumericLimits</strong> is a tool class used to query attributes such as the maximum and minimum values of a specified data type.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row154197811816">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2094712054713"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0188.html">GetTaskRatio</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29729544501">Applies to the separated Cube/Vector mode and obtains the Cube/Vector ratio.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12385124516283">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17516125692817"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0189.html">GetUBSizeInBytes</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17516165612819"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph251617564281">Obtains the UB size, in bytes.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row34503263319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p543714258341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00190.html">GetRuntimeUBSize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9464325332"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph167621637909">Obtains the runtime UB size, in bytes.</span> You can calculate the number of cycles based on the UB size.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table2017815711517" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 12 </b>Kernel tiling APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11179357358">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" id="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p417975719517">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" id="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1817910573513">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14180457256">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1518025711512"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0214.html">GET_TILING_DATA</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p818095714515">Obtains the tiling information input by the kernel entry point function of the operator and fills the information in the registered tiling structure. This function is compiled in macro expansion mode. If a user has registered multiple TilingData structures, this API is used to return the default registered structure.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row165593514011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p155603514401"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0215.html">GET_TILING_DATA_WITH_STRUCT</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10569356405">Specifies a structure name to obtain the specified tiling information and fills the information in the corresponding tiling structure. This function is compiled in macro expansion mode.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row146553974019">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p246512399407"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0216.html">GET_TILING_DATA_MEMBER</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18466133917406">Obtains the member variables of a tiling structure.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row818045715513">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1318010571658"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0217.html">TILING_KEY_IS</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p41805570511">Checks whether the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b31310291111357">tiling_key</strong> in the current kernel function execution is equal to a specific key, so as to identify a kernel branch with <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b129458343511357">tiling_key==key</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row930185314421">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1830185311424"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00003.html">REGISTER_TILING_DEFAULT</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p63075319421">Registers the default TilingData structure defined by the user using the standard C++ syntax on the kernel.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2047417552427">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1147415594219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00004.html">REGISTER_TILING_FOR_TILINGKEY</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001526206862_p0824144014589">Registers a custom TilingData structure that matches the TilingKey on the kernel. This API requires a logical expression that uses the string <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b996314261713">TILING_KEY_VAR</strong> to represent the actual TilingKey and the range that the TilingKey satisfies.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8142114933417">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p187745415347"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00164.html">REGISTER_NONE_TILING</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1131075619124">When using a custom TilingData structure defined with the standard C++ syntax in the kernel, if you are unsure which structures need to be registered, you can use this API to inform the framework that you need to use the unregistered standard C++ syntax to define the TilingData. This API works in conjunction with <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0215.html">GET_TILING_DATA_WITH_STRUCT</a>, <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0216.html">GET_TILING_DATA_MEMBER</a>, and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00140.html">GET_TILING_DATA_PTR_WITH_STRUCT</a> to obtain the corresponding TilingData.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row131802571959">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p51807572055"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0218.html">KERNEL_TASK_TYPE_DEFAULT</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1618045718520">Sets the global default kernel type, which applies to all tiling keys.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row21801457154">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0180185713514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0218.html">KERNEL_TASK_TYPE</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p201806571756">Sets the kernel type corresponding to a specific tiling key.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table19526741203211" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 13 </b>ISASI APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row352624118322">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" id="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p88065174816">Category</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14526241173218">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" id="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p145261141203210">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1216412458149">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p259453711267">Scalar Computation</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5421318131512"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00089.html">WriteGmByPassDCache</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p204221851520"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph94221821518">Writes data to the global memory address without passing through the DCache.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row69501477146">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p74217189157"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00090.html">ReadGmByPassDCache</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19422018191516"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph14211188156">Reads data from the global memory address without passing through the DCache.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row154401144114718">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="15" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12441154416479">Vector Computation</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20441544114710"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0221.html">VectorPadding</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p244134434718">Performs the padding operation on the source operand by the data block based on <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b54003963542611">padMode</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b41133535142611">padSide</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10526441173211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1994405814488"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0222.html">BilinearInterpolation</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1526144123212">Performs bilinear interpolation operations, including vertical iteration and horizontal iteration.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1952734143214">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99449586481"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0223.html">GetCmpMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1152720414327">Obtains the comparison result of the <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0067.html">Compare (Result Stored in a Register)</a> instruction.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row946564134915">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5465104110496"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0224.html">SetCmpMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p184655418496">Sets the comparison register for the APIs where <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0070.html">Select</a> does not specify the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b85509642142611">mask</strong> parameter.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1952774119326">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8429183014919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0225.html">GetReduceRepeatSumSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165272418326">Obtains the computation result of <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0078.html">ReduceSum</a> (based on the first <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i1701562201">n</em> data elements of a tensor).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row125271041103210">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12638205318500"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0226.html">GetReduceRepeatMaxMinSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p195452267225">Obtains the maximum/minimum values and the corresponding index values in the scenario where <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0076.html">ReduceMax</a> and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0077.html">ReduceMin</a> are consecutive.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row185271941123212">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p169441258134818"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0227.html">ProposalConcat</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14527241173218">Inserts consecutive elements into the corresponding positions in Region Proposals. In each iteration, 16 consecutive elements are inserted into the corresponding positions in 16 Region Proposals.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13527134173211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39441158184816"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0228.html">ProposalExtract</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10462291478">Extracts elements from corresponding positions in Region Proposals and rearranges them. In each iteration, 16 elements are extracted from 16 Region Proposals and arranged consecutively. The functionality of this API is the opposite of that of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1743311485421">ProposalConcat</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row452716417321">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6944165817489"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0229.html">RpSort16</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p773018265170">Sorts the Region Proposals based on their <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b124349556911357">score</strong> fields in descending order. 16 Region Proposals are sorted in each iteration.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row745884215480">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139441358114819"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0230.html">MrgSort4</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101781237141713">Merges up to four sorted Region Proposal lists into one. The results are sorted in descending order of the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b188020537711357">score</strong> fields.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1019917563485">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p594585811484"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0231.html">Sort32</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p151991556204813">Serves as a sorting function that can sort up to 32 elements in each iteration.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18337125610483">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10945175864811"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0232.html">MrgSort</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p78578171182">Merges up to four sorted lists into one. The results are sorted in descending order of the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b59935006811357">score</strong> fields.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8465195684815">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139456584485"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0233.html">GetMrgSortResult</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101531828111815">Obtains the number of region proposals in the queue processed by <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0232.html">MrgSort</a> or <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0230.html">MrgSort4</a> and stores the number in the four List input parameters in sequence.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row46098568481">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1794575816483"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0234.html">Gatherb</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4609556174812">Gathers a given input tensor to the result tensor based on the offset address tensor provided.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row576405615486">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1945158154815"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0235.html">Scatter</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1836365951815">With a given contiguous input tensor and a destination address offset tensor, generates a new result tensor based on the offset address and distributes the input tensor to the result tensor.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row55961050102215">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="8" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3596650162219">Vector Computation</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p94614512565"></p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4570181317114"></p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p162501316917"></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1627401015234"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00245.html">Prelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17596550132219"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph171052419253">Writes <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562461141_b3623113614411">src0</strong> to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562461141_b207321242124119">dst</strong> directly if <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562461141_b365416476416">src0</strong> is greater than 0. Otherwise, writes the result of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562461141_b657526134216">src0 × src1</strong> to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562461141_b1347238194214">dst</strong>.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10490181412234">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p768371913238"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00246.html">Mull</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17491151412313"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph711422918258">Multiplies the first <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002562301889_i47039555817">count</em> data elements of src0 and src1 element-wise, and writes the result to dst0Local. If the result overflows, the overflow part is written to dst1Local.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row610542433612">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17573105103717"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00253.html">AbsSub</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1310512414366"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4885524194011">Subtracts <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381714_b20553714143414">src1</strong> from <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381714_b6553121418346">src0Local</strong>, takes the absolute value, and stores the result to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381714_b195531114123418">dst</strong>.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row21531226203613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p968312193716"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00254.html">ExpSub</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15153126143614"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph2080081312419">Subtracts <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531541950_b82871722114417">src1</strong> from <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531541950_b152871322194419">src0</strong>, uses the difference as the exponent to compute the power of the natural constant <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531541950_b4879155116463">e</strong>, and stores the result to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531541950_b112875227441">dst</strong>.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18177628103613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p128991739174417"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0049.html">MulsCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p121781328103612"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph17644543174116">Multiplies the first <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381704_i928731559">count</em> elements of the vector source operand by a scalar, converts the result to half type in CAST_ROUND mode, and stores the final result to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000002531381704_b228153155515">dst</strong>. The scalar can be before or after the tensor.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11469585616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p522018118566"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00258.html">Truncate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p74618519564"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph12508132815572">Truncates floating-point elements of the source operand to their integer part, while retaining the data type of the source operand.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row195707131411">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1434016281613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00036.html">Interleave</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p657010139118"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph664518360219">Interleaves the elements from given source operands src0 and src1 and stores them in the result operands dst0 and dst1.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19250111610113">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p184274411111"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00037.html">DeInterleave</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p22501166119"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph054752519311">Deinterleaves the elements from given source operands src0 and src1 and stores them in the result operands dst0 and dst1.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row224111331412">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p711814301143">Data Movement</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p148821245184915"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0265.html">DataCopyPad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p019795716484">Enables data non-aligned movement.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7375515151412">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p148821245194911"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0266.html">SetPadValue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6689192111598">Sets the value filled by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b17301131244714">DataCopyPad</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row154051221164915">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="26" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p118921556154813">Cube Computation</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1255212302496"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0249.html">Mmad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24861371569">Performs matrix multiplication and addition.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12765526165715">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1176511267573"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0250.html">MmadWithSparse</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p47651826105712">Performs matrix multiplication and addition operations. The input left matrix A is a sparse matrix, and the input right matrix B is a dense matrix.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row0435522154918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8552203094910"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p64161631122120">Sets register values. This API is similar to <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a>, <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0260.html">SetMMRowMajor</a>, and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00197.html">SetMMColumnMajor</a>. <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b177480575011357">SetHF32Mode</strong> is used to set the HF32 mode of the MMAD.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1858982214918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165526308490"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1478477185019">Sets register values. This API is similar to <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a>, <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0260.html">SetMMRowMajor</a>, and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00197.html">SetMMColumnMajor</a>. <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b76513502311357">SetHF32TransMode</strong> is used to set the HF32 rounding mode of the MMAD. It is valid only when the HF32 mode of the MMAD takes effect.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1374212216499">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19945205916295"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0260.html">SetMMRowMajor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p87421422144917">Sets register values. This API is similar to <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a> and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a>. This API sets MMAD computation to traverse preferentially along the N dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row9217141164613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14218134119466"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00197.html">SetMMColumnMajor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18317459134619">Sets register values. This API is similar to <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a> and <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a>. This API sets MMAD computation to traverse preferentially along the M dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19411232491">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20552113020499"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0262.html">Conv2D</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10261152153414">Performs 2D convolution on a given input tensor and a weight tensor and outputs a result tensor. The Conv2d convolution layer is mostly used for image recognition, and a filter is used to extract features in an image.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row144112573483">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9552930204920"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0263.html">Gemm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14116577483">Multiplies two tensors and outputs a result tensor. Multiply matrix A and matrix B to obtain matrix C, and output matrix C.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row147451115151916">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p45523300491"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0252.html">SetFixPipeConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17851154862112">Sets the tensor quantization parameters for the real-time quantization during <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a> (CO1 -&gt; GM or CO1 -&gt; A1).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row294174016209">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p65522030174916"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0253.html">SetFixpipeNz2ndFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2898124417263">Sets the NZ2ND configuration for the real-time format conversion (NZ2ND) during <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a> (CO1 -&gt; GM or CO1 -&gt; A1).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11129104162017">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165521930144913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0254.html">SetFixpipePreQuantFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16332111515234">Sets the scalar quantization parameters for the real-time quantization process during <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a> (CO1 -&gt; GM or CO1 -&gt; A1).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19331114117200">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p278152219274"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0255.html">SetFixPipeClipRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10787226279">Sets the maximum value of the ClipReLU operation after real-time quantization during <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a> (CO1 -&gt; GM).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row0541441152019">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1674634112715"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0256.html">SetFixPipeAddr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p71911024132712">Sets the address of the LocalTensor for the element-wise operation after real-time quantization during <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a> (CO1-&gt;GM).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row08411461812">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1955143017499"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0237.html">Fill</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1489215634815">Initializes LocalTensor (TPosition: A1, A2, B1, or B2) to a specific value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row696114470313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p455133074917"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0238.html">LoadData</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3448142813492">Provides the Load2D and Load3D data loading functions.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8181746635">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20551143074919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0239.html">LoadDataWithTranspose</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p124281245667">Loads 2D data with transposing from A1/B1 to A2/B2.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row194791339636">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12211416165318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0240.html">SetAippFunctions</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p192129164538">Sets AI preprocessing (AIPP) parameters for images.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1948463715319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1061103015541"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0241.html">LoadImageToLocal</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p792614613548">Transfers image data from the GM to A1/B1. During the transfer, you can preprocess images, including image flipping, image resizing (clipping, cropping, scaling, and stretching), color space conversion (CSC), and type conversion.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row0248535339">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16995181110545"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0242.html">LoadUnZipIndex</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20995111113541">Loads the compression index table on the GM to the internal register.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row132838331033">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1434981445411"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0243.html">LoadDataUnzip</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2349121415547">Decompress the data on the GM and transfer the data to A1, B1, and B2.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7838730733">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1418115368566"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0244.html">LoadDataWithSparse</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7263112805617">Moves the 512-byte dense weight matrix stored in B1 to B2, and reads the 128-byte index matrix for sparseness of the dense matrix.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row66591928736">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1552163012493"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0245.html">SetFmatrix</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1365632015497">Sets the attribute description of the feature map when <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0238.html">Load3Dv1/Load3Dv2</a> is called.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row07692515218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p055233018498"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0246.html">SetLoadDataBoundary</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p37801058115614">Sets A1/B1 boundary value when <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0238.html">Load3D</a> is called.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3787671224">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1155273074915"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0247.html">SetLoadDataRepeat</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p532619442559">Sets the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b193581124211357">repeat</strong> parameter of the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b92679597911357">Load3Dv2</strong> API. After the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b122272320911357">repeat</strong> parameter is set, the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b141976387311357">Load3Dv2</strong> API can be called once to complete the data movement for multiple iterations.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4662691428">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18552183094913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0248.html">SetLoadDataPaddingValue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p53183549554">Sets <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b168300373511357">padValue</strong> for <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b68388498111357">Load3Dv1</strong>/<strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b185989968211357">Load3Dv2</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row165019119218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12552123034913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0251.html">Fixpipe</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7194151162615">Processes the result after the matrix computation is complete. For example, the computation result is quantized and the data is moved from CO1 to the Global Memory.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row188711975503">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="5" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13495131445016">Synchronization Control</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14495181465015"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0270.html">SetFlag/WaitFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p158718715503">Synchronizes different pipelines in the same core. This synchronization operation needs to be inserted between different pipeline instructions with data dependency.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row181116835017">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16495161412505"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0271.html">PipeBarrier</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p21128145011">Blocks a pipeline. This synchronization operation needs to be inserted between the same pipelines with data dependency.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row653814222592">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1289326165918"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0272.html">DataSyncBarrier</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8600163212594">Blocks the execution of subsequent instructions until all previous memory access instructions (the memory location to be waited for can be controlled by parameters) are executed.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row114551284506">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5495141475017"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0273.html">CrossCoreSetFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20555155242412">Sets the synchronization between the Cube Core (AIC) and Vector Core (AIV) on the AI Core for the separated mode.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1169128105014">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1495141410509"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0274.html">CrossCoreWaitFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1453712312015">Waits for the synchronization between the Cube Core (AIC) and Vector Core (AIV) on the AI Core for the separated mode.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13905175871">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123688281178">Synchronization Control</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99126441977"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00132.html">Mutex</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1190519517715"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1520973121010">Mutex is used for synchronization between asynchronous pipeline instructions in a core. Its function is similar to the lock mechanism in traditional CPUs. The synchronization dependency between pipelines is implemented by locking specified pipelines and then releasing them. Each lock has a fixed mutex ID, which can be custom (ranging from 0 to 27) or released using <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00136.html">AllocMutexID/ReleaseMutexID</a>.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7712137571">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p532817527716"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00136.html">AllocMutexID</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p147126719714">Obtains and occupies a MutexID from the framework. This API is used together with <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00137.html">ReleaseMutexID</a> to manage the obtaining and release of MutexIDs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5734121020712">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1989145819720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00137.html">ReleaseMutexID</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1973571020715"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph10161191691011">Releases a MutexID from the framework. It is used together with <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00136.html">AllocMutexID</a>.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row329139125011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p164958141507">Cache Processing</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p124961914135016"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0276.html">ICachePreLoad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16327940182218">Preloads instructions to the iCache from the DDR address where the instructions are located.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19196799505">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4496131419504"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0277.html">GetICachePreloadStatus</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p319669165012">Obtains the PreLoad status of the iCache.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2493696509">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p649661485013">System Variable Access</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1649651485012"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0279.html">GetProgramCounter</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1349315905014">Obtains the pointer to the program counter, which is used to record the current program execution position.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19631896502">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p74961814175010"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0280.html">GetSubBlockNum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p966612452211">Obtains the number of Vector Cores on the AI Core.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row278314916501">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2496314115019"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0281.html">GetSubBlockIdx</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7322151817114">Obtains the ID of the Vector Core on the AI Core.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row591914913509">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p449621416508"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0282.html">GetSystemCycle</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p318519518103">Obtains the number of cycles in the current system. If the number of cycles is converted to time (unit: μs), the frequency must be 50 MHz. The conversion formula is as follows: Time = (Number of cycles/50) μs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row23644391720">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13856134291819">System Variable Access</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p236104361711"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00091.html">SetCtrlSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123610436177"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1641093112018">Sets specific bits of the CTRL register (control register).</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row191522045131718">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p186893131181"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00092.html">GetCtrlSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17152114561719"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1892618377202">Reads the value of a specific bit in the CTRL register (control register).</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row28763469172">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p876017348182"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00093.html">ResetCtrlSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5876194619170"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph818454202">Resets specific bits of the CTRL register (control register).</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row20229161014506">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p649691414505">Atomic Operations</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p114961614195013"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0284.html">SetAtomicMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1555107145615">Sets whether to perform atomic comparison for subsequent data transferred from VECOUT to GM, which compares the content to be copied with the existing content in GM and writes the maximum value to GM.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row237920103503">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18496141413508"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0285.html">SetAtomicMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15379191018502">Sets whether to perform atomic comparison for subsequent data transferred from VECOUT to GM, which compares the content to be copied with the existing content in GM and writes the minimum value to GM.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5515201016501">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4496814165013"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0286.html">SetStoreAtomicConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p95151510125017">Sets the atomic operation enabling flag and type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row466110104502">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p949614146509"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0287.html">GetStoreAtomicConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4576142854016">Obtains the value of the enabling flag and type of the atomic operation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row161503191126">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1715151915126">Debugging APIs</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9552163084912"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0261.html">CheckLocalMemoryIA</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5863853185711">Monitors the UB read and write operations within the specified range. If the UB read and write operations within the specified range are monitored, an EXCEPTION error is reported. If the UB read and write operations within the specified range are not monitored, no error is reported.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18446920142913">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p164464202295">Cube Group Management</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7499133216292"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0289.html">CubeResGroupHandle</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24466202298">Controls communication between the AIC and the AIV through soft synchronization in the separated mode, to implement <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__en-us_topic_0000001588832845_ph168139148536">AI Core</span> compute resource grouping.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1493333852918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8398498294"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0301.html">GroupBarrier</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1493314380295">When two AIV tasks in the same <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0289.html">CubeResGroupHandle</a> object depend on each other, control synchronization by calling <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b81544121519">GroupBarrier</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1786094112299">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p186215598290"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0307.html">KfcWorkspace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1776114192019"><strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b16991123156">KfcWorkspace</strong> is the communication workspace descriptor, which is used to manage the message communication area division of different <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0289.html">CubeResGroupHandle</a> objects. It is used together with <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b2991823354">CubeResGroupHandle</strong>. The <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b77117246410">KfcWorkspace</strong> constructor is used to create a <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b27117245420">KfcWorkspace</strong> object.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>
   <div class="section" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section3317105813235">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">High-Level APIs</h4>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table6328746161212" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 14 </b>Mathematical computation APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18328114610121">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" id="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p173281846121219">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" id="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p232844620126">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7328204651217">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2032844612121"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0504.html">Acos</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17328164651218">Computes arc cosine element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19328124671211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17328204661218"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0564.html">Acosh</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p83298462120">Computes inverse hyperbolic cosine element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11329946171211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p63297464128"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0496.html">Asin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20329114641219">Computes arcsine element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1632954617129">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3329546101216"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0560.html">Asinh</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p932994611216">Computes the hyperbolic arcsine element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1329646131216">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10329184619129"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0516.html">Atan</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p232904617122">Computes the arc tangent of a trigonometric function element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1932944621219">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">

       </tr>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1032984631216"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0585.html">Axpy</a></p></td>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8329114612121">Adds the product of each element of the source operand and a scalar to the corresponding element in the destination operand.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row732916466122">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1432924610129">Obtains the minimum integer value greater than or equal to <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i52006699511357">x</em>, that is, rounding towards positive infinity.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row232994671216">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14329104681213">Replaces the number greater than scalar with scalar in srcTensor and retains the number less than or equal to scalar as the dstTensor output.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row53290468122">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1132984620124">Replaces the number less than scalar with scalar in srcTensor and retains the number greater than or equal to scalar as the dstTensor output.</p></td>
       </tr>

       </tr>

       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p333019462124">Computes the logarithmic derivative of the gamma function of x element-wise.</p></td>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13330204601215">Returns the complementary error function computing result of input <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i132258947911357">x</em>. The integral ranges from <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i11876908911357">x</em> to infinity.</p></td>
