        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1033024691219">Obtains the minimum integer value less than or equal to <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i27541312611357">x</em>, that is, rounding towards negative infinity.</p></td>
       </tr>

       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p071135951519">Computes the square root of the sum of squares of two floating-point numbers element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11981225203513">Performs element-wise checks to determine whether the input floating-point numbers are neither NaN nor ±INF.</p></td>
       </tr>

       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5331204620122">Computes logarithm of bases e, 2, and 10 element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7331194661216">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11331946151214">Rounds the input element to the nearest integer.</p></td>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p433110466122">Performs the Sign operation element-wise. Sign refers to the symbol that returns the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>

       </tr>

       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20331144651218">Truncates floating point numbers element-wise, that is, rounding towards zero.</p></td>
       </tr>

       </tr>

       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p38041818296">Performs element-wise checks to determine whether the input floating-point numbers are NaN.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13670181019299">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p06701810122920">Performs element-wise checks to determine whether the input floating-point numbers are ±INF.</p></td>
       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1474571382914">Obtains the integer closest to the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17877815152917">Performs sine and cosine computation element-wise to obtain the sine and cosine results.</p></td>
       </tr>

       </tr>

       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5448341105615">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5448204112567"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10256.html">LogicalOrs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p194481841115610">Performs a logical OR operation between each element in an input vector and a scalar.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4668163093217">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p187781634193218"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10277.html">LogicalXor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p147786344321">Performs the logical exclusive OR operation by element.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5422125043317">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p186138413415"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10265.html">BitwiseNot</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p861494153417">Performs a bitwise NOT operation on the input.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3119242135615">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1111924235618"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10249.html">BitwiseAnd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20119174210565">Performs a bitwise AND operation on two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1847385613338">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p155221232342"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10269.html">BitwiseOr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4522152393416">Performs a bitwise OR operation on two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row394945893311">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10748540103412"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10273.html">BitwiseXor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p974816402341">Performs a bitwise XOR operation on two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row36751142135614">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20675134217564"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10260.html">Where</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7675194211563">Selects elements from two source operands based on specified conditions to generate a target operand.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

      <thead align="left">

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" id="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row101065443561">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1710674455615"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10166.html">AntiQuantize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1910684465613">Performs element-wise fake quantization. For example, apply fake quantization to quantize int8_t to half.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row164273359136">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p742783581318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0822.html">AscendAntiQuant</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5427835121317">Performs element-wise fake quantization. For example, apply fake quantization to quantize int8_t to half.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row87731046155619">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p167731446135611"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10164.html">Dequantize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9773446115615">Performs element-wise dequantization. For example, it can dequantize int32_t to half or float.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12427435111311">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1642733591318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0820.html">AscendDequant</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11427635151312">Performs dequantization by element. For example, dequantize the int32_t data type to the half/float data type.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18942184911567">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1494254905613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10168.html">Quantize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7942134917563">Performs element-wise quantization. For example, it can quantize half or float into int8_t.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row164271635201317">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2427435201319"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0818.html">AscendQuant</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p342718351139">Performs quantization by element. For example, quantize the half/float data type to the int8_t data type.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>

      <caption>

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12781510111418">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" id="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12782101020142">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" id="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6782101016142">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8786410121418">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4786131012145"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0806.html">BatchNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1778631013141">Normalizes each input feature of samples in each batch along the batch dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row87861710151413">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11786410151410"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0808.html">DeepNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17861410111419">Serves as a replacement for LayerNorm normalization during the training process of a deep neural network.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18506114553319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p571415485334"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0816.html">GroupNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11714174853314">Divides the input C dimension into groups (<em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i18161201419538">groupNum</em>) and standardizes each group of data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5786191091412">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11786131071415"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0797.html">LayerNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p157861910161416">Normalizes the input data of network layers to the [0, 1] range to standardize the distributions of both input and output data across network layers.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row378616105148">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p47863104149"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0797.html">LayerNormGrad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11786141091411">Computes the backpropagation gradient of LayerNorm.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7786111071411">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1978721091412"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0799.html">LayerNormGradBeta</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p978771018141">Obtains the reverse beta/gmma value and outputs pdx, gmma, and beta when used in conjunction with LayerNormGrad.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1833102211208">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p233172292010"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0810.html">Normalize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p33482202020">Computes the reciprocal <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b12635938165310">rstd</strong> of the standard deviation of the input data with shape [A, R] and the normalized output <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b102071052145310">y</strong> based on the known mean value and variance in <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0797.html">LayerNorm</a>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row478741061416">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7787121071417"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0804.html">RmsNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19787171017144">Normalizes input data whose shape is [B, S, H] using RmsNorm.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row48116253209">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p58111925192011"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0812.html">WelfordUpdate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p48111625102019">Implements preprocessing of the Welford algorithm.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row189721927192015">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29731627182019"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0814.html">WelfordFinalize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p69731627112018">Implements postprocessing of the Welford algorithm.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p135235061516"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0760.html">AdjustSoftMaxRes</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1952380121514">Performs postprocessing on SoftMax compute results and adjusts SoftMax compute results to specified values.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10523408156">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15232011159">Implements an activation function of the simplified FastGelu version.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row452380181515">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p552413017153">Implements an activation function of the FastGeluV2 version.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16524110151518">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1952430161515"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0786.html">GeGLU</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165241407155">Serves as a GLU variant that uses GeLU as the activation function.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row65248014151">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p252412091510"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0771.html">Gelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p175241406158">Serves as an important activation function that is inspired by ReLU and dropout. The idea of random regularization is introduced in activation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row165241301159">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p155243071519"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0768.html">LogSoftMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p35248041516">Performs LogSoftmax computation on the input tensor.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1352419071515">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1152410051518"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0790.html">ReGlu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p75248013150">Serves as a GLU variant that uses ReLU as the activation function.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row852413013150">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p852415071513"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0793.html">Sigmoid</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1052419031510">Performs logistic regression with Sigmoid element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1252416071515">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p25241601158"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0780.html">Silu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p852416041517">Computes Silu element-wise.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5524190141518">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p852419019156"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0755.html">SimpleSoftMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p35247012154">Uses the computed sum and max data to perform softmax computation on the input tensor.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1752419071511">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p195249051510"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0754.html">SoftMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16525100121512">Performs softmax computation on input tensors by row.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row452512011153">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p205251707159"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0756.html">SoftmaxFlash</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1152511081511">Serves as the enhanced version of SoftMax. It not only performs softmaxflash computation on the input tensor but also updates the result of the current softmax computation based on the sum and max values obtained in the previous softmax computation.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row352510091518">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p65251081518"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0758.html">SoftmaxFlashV2</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p175251107154">Serves as the enhanced version of SoftmaxFlash, corresponding to the FlashAttention-2 algorithm.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row46249508349">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10625550163411"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10001.html">SoftmaxFlashV3</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p662545013412">Serves as the enhanced version of SoftmaxFlash, corresponding to the Softmax PASA algorithm.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row65251015155">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p155254018158"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0757.html">SoftmaxGrad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8525507154">Performs gradient backpropagation on input tensors.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row152513016156">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p352512001517"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0759.html">SoftmaxGradFront</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p752570131513">Performs gradient backpropagation on input tensors.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row135257081512">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9525200111511"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0776.html">SwiGLU</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1952550131511">Serves as a GLU variant that uses Swish as the activation function.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1252515041520">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p752515071514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0783.html">Swish</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p95251306151">Serves as a Swish activation function in neural networks.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13688203811515">Obtains the sum of elements in the last dimension.</p></td>

       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row106871338111517">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1668715385158">Computes the mean of elements according to the direction of the last axis.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row186871038171510">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12688163811519">Performs the XOR (bitwise XOR) operation by element and computes the sum of the results using <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b2018515093117">ReduceSum</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11517441717">Accumulates data of a multi-dimensional vector based on a specified dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row199410371158">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6979134616516">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p197934616510">Returns the maximum value of a multi-dimensional vector along a specified dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2429249255">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p164291497518">Returns the minimum value of a multi-dimensional vector along a specified dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1211653851">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15211153654">Computes the logical OR of a multi-dimensional vector along a specified dimension.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18411155515512">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.6.2.3.1.1 ">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row475745191616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p875865181616"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0836.html">TopK</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p97581255164">Obtains the first <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b160457138911357">k</strong> maximum or minimum values of the last dimension and their corresponding indexes.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row97581516168">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1475812517162"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0839.html">Concat</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p375875131613">Preprocesses the data and merges the source operand srcLocal to be sorted into the target data concatLocal. After the data is preprocessed, you can sort the data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14758650165">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p87581950161">Processes the sorting result data and outputs the sorted values and indexes.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row57583513169">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1375813514166"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0842.html">Sort</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14758554166">Sorts data in descending order by value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row475815512168">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17581591616"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0847.html">MrgSort</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1375885121617">Merges up to four sorted lists into one. The results are sorted in descending order of the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b17618355811357">score</strong> fields.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1943972012455">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p138751045194610"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0859.html">Select</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0875445174619">Given two source operands <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b16106145171915">src0</strong> and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b71061851191920">src1</strong>, selects elements based on the values (non-bit) of corresponding positions of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b510618519199">maskTensor</strong> to obtain the destination operand <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b10106751201910">dst</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row193983120178">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.6.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p103981111179"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0862.html">DropOut</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.6.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20398121101710">Provides the function of filtering the source operand based on the mask tensor to obtain the destination operand.</p></td>
       </tr>

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.33%" id="mcps1.3.6.9.2.3.1.2">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.33%" headers="mcps1.3.6.9.2.3.1.2 ">

       </tr>

       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row690975463312">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p189091354173316">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row209091454163314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1690965443315">Waits until all threads in the current thread block have reached this function.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1390945433317">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1590925411337"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10307.html">asc_threadfence</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139091542339">Ensures the time sequence of write operations when different cores access the same global or shared memory.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1415719381045">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101577387413"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10449.html">asc_threadfence_block</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14158138445">Coordinates the memory operation sequence of threads in the same thread block to ensure that all memory read and write operations before a thread calls <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1688551153316">asc_threadfence_block ()</strong> are visible to other threads in the same thread block.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10301542341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10462.html">normcdfinvf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15301346347">Obtains the inverse function of the standard normal cumulative distribution of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b687712148482">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16915105413334">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p891555443311">Obtains the value of the zeroth-order first-type Bessel function j0 of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b186451417164815">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1191545463312">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1891595418336">Obtains the value of the first-order first-type Bessel function j1 of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b25331128194810">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row99153549332">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2091595483312"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10370.html">jnf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12915454173316">Obtains the value of the <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i3359834124820">n</em>th-order first-type Bessel function j<em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i143592348482">n</em> of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b4359153414811">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1191565416337">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10915185414338"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10371.html">y0f</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p991514545332">Obtains the value of the zeroth-order second-type Bessel function y0 of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b96883531489">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8915185420338">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29151854203311"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10372.html">y1f</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13915155483317">Obtains the value of the first-order second-type Bessel function y1 of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b8807515513">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row491595483318">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19915165418337"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10373.html">ynf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p189151754133313">Obtains the value of the <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i169226718517">n</em>th-order second-type Bessel function y<em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i592219719518">n</em> of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b179226711516">x</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5915354113314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14916175416338"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10463.html">fabsf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p119161754193319">Obtains the absolute value of the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1345410171828">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1454617720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10464.html">__habs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p134549171823">Obtains the absolute value of the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row189161954123314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p991615546339"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10465.html">fmaf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3916105413333">Computes the result of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b12787203025213">x</strong> multiplied by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b5787430155211">y</strong> plus <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b20787630145219">z</strong> for the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6787193019525">x</strong>, <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1578733013528">y</strong>, and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b37870303521">z</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row189014232040">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99017231749"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10466.html">__hfma</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p390152312411">Computes the result of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b167329457525">x</strong> multiplied by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b157328451525">y</strong> plus <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b67323452529">z</strong> for the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b18732134525215">x</strong>, <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1773217459526">y</strong>, and <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6732945185211">z</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1891611541336">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2916115413339"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10467.html">fmaxf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p091616545335">Obtains the maximum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row311020351658">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p81110351658"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10468.html">__hmax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16734242758">Obtains the maximum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row139161854153314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p291645411338"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10469.html">fminf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p891675463312">Obtains the minimum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1458293819518">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1658211381353"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10470.html">__hmin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7582438957">Obtains the minimum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15916454203311">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4916115411332"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10471.html">fdimf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13916125433311">Obtains the difference between two inputs. If the difference is less than 0, 0 is returned.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row119165548334">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12916195415332"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10472.html">remquof</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1791695411333">Obtains the remainder of input <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b112426975312">x</strong> divided by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1324216995315">y</strong>. The quotient is rounded to the nearest integer. If the floating-point result of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b181662253532">x</strong> divided by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1916612535319">y</strong> is exactly halfway between two integers, the quotient is rounded to the even integer. The quotient is then stored in the variable pointed to by the pointer <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b71661825165316">quo</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13916254203313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14916254133316"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10473.html">fmodf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p491615453316">Obtains the remainder of input <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b379582314538">x</strong> divided by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b17795182310538">y</strong>. The quotient is the integer part of the floating-point result of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b587917476536">x</strong> divided by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1879184713532">y</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1791614546337">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139161541336"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10474.html">remainderf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5916185418337">Obtains the remainder of input <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b3796423115318">x</strong> divided by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1379662355310">y</strong>. The quotient is rounded to the nearest integer. If the floating-point result of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b174749537533">x</strong> divided by <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1547475365319">y</strong> is exactly halfway between two integers, the quotient is rounded to the even integer.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row091635423313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39166542332"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10475.html">copysignf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p209161154133315">Obtains a floating-point number composed of the magnitude of the first input <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b9326195635310">x</strong> and the sign of the second input <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1232675619531">y</strong>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12916105473310">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1091665433317"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10476.html">nearbyIntf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16917354163318">Obtains the integer closest to the input floating-point number. If the input floating-point number is exactly halfway between two integers, the even integer is returned.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row129171654113313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p191775483318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10477.html">nextafterf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18917185418334">If <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b758815175416">y</strong> is greater than <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1358825205416">x</strong>, the function returns the next representable floating-point value larger than <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6588125105411">x</strong>, that is, incrementing the least significant bit of the floating-point number's binary representation by one.</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8917354133310">If <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1654172419543">y</strong> is less than <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b565472412549">x</strong>, the function returns the next representable floating-point value less than <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1165472435414">x</strong>, that is, decrementing the least significant bit of the floating-point number's binary representation by one.</p>
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1991745433314">If <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6463636185419">y</strong> is equal to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b546383645410">x</strong>, <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b164631836195420">x</strong> is returned.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row149171154153319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p169175549335"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10478.html">scalbnf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5917135413314">Obtains the product of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b7361143985412">x</strong> and 2 raised to the power of <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i1361183917547">n</em>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3917125453319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4917125413310"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10479.html">scalblnf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p119172054133314">Obtains the product of the input data <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b9339849115410">x</strong> and 2 raised to the power of <em id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__i3339174919542">n</em>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row178323439144">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7832443201414"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10480.html">modff</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p883214361417">Decomposes the input data into the fractional part and the integer part.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row21937201350">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p719313201519"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10481.html">labs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1419316201258">Obtains the absolute value of the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2077317477520">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1377314471756"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10482.html">llabs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p157737471356">Obtains the absolute value of the input data.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row07291552354">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p107292521754"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10483.html">llmax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14729752759">Obtains the maximum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row03165507517">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p103167505515"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10484.html">ullmax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p183163501555">Obtains the maximum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11648735153">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5648163513514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10485.html">umax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p76484350516">Obtains the maximum value between two inputs.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1985110321853">

       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14502052114216">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p05018528422">Reverses the bit order of the input data and returns the reversed value.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19257175012426">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1725775054211"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10497.html">__clz</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7257205004217">Counts the number of consecutive leading zeros in the binary representation of the input data, starting from the most significant bit (MSB).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6981151974314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20981121911432"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10498.html">__ffs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8981111964314">Scans the binary representation of the input data starting from the least significant bit (LSB) and returns the index (1-based) of the first bit set to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b148105817716">1</strong>. If no bits are set to 1, it returns 0.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1249114232433">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14911823104318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10499.html">__popc</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14491723184311">Counts the number of bits set to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b4283193421016">1</strong> in the binary representation of the input data, starting from the MSB to the LSB.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8756162115437">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19756821194314"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10500.html">__byte_perm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p167564212438">Combines two 4-byte uint32_t inputs into an 8-byte 64-bit integer. The selector s specifies which 4 bytes to select, and these 4 bytes are concatenated into a uint32_t integer from LSB to MSB.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p874412316911"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10416.html">__hisnan</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15744113114918">Checks whether a floating-point number is nan.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5396191822512">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p93961918152514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10417.html">isinf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18215105443017">Checks whether a floating-point number is infinite.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3972933196">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p199724331919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10418.html">__hisinf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19728332911">Checks whether a floating-point number is infinite.</p></td>
       </tr>

    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table17209165495117" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 32 </b>Atomic functions
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row720915541514">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16210954205119">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p122101254105114">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row221025405119">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18210165415117"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10375.html">asc_atomic_add</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14210105415119">Performs an atomic add operation on the data in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph10536132505718">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1753616252577">Global Memory</span> and the specified data, accumulating the specified data into the data in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph15143152082811">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph214322082812">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row102101054135111">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10210654105113"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10376.html">asc_atomic_sub</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1021019548518">Performs an atomic subtract operation on the data in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph5434440195718">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1343414075710">Global Memory</span> and the specified data, subtracting the specified data from the data in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph93051920194612">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1330532015466">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14210155416511">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12101954125120"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10377.html">asc_atomic_exch</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p921017541514">Performs an atomic assignment operation on the address in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph19824155135715">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph2824251175717">Global Memory</span>, assigning the specified data to the address in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph38242515577">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1782418514572">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row9210125445116">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16210654145112"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10378.html">asc_atomic_max</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p182101754115113">Performs an atomic maximum operation on the data in <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph82259014584">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph722514018587">Global Memory</span>, assigning the maximum value between the data in <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph72255035818">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph3225180185819">Global Memory</span> and the specified data to the address in <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph16444335125614">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph2444133513562">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5210165425115">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p321035415115"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10379.html">asc_atomic_min</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16210155445111">Performs an atomic minimum operation on the data in <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph236151245817">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph17361012165811">Global Memory</span>, assigning the minimum value between the data in <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph103671216583">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph43616124587">Global Memory</span> and the specified data to the address in <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1336191245816">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1236121265812">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row162101254175110">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p421011547518"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10380.html">asc_atomic_inc</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0210115417511">Performs an atomic increment operation on the address value in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4991141914580">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph18991719175819">Global Memory</span>. If the address value is greater than or equal to the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b168981816153218">val</strong>, the address value is set to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b68984168326">0</strong>. Otherwise, the address value is incremented by 1.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19210145435112">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2210115455112"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10381.html">asc_atomic_dec</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1021025445117">Performs an atomic decrement operation on the address value in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1959983155818">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph05991831195817">Global Memory</span>. If the address value is 0 or greater than the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b175991549339">val</strong>, the address value is set to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b860094113313">val</strong>. Otherwise, the address value is decremented by 1.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13198193011527">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p48379327525"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10382.html">asc_atomic_cas</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101941330125214">Performs an atomic compare-and-swap operation on the address value in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph8959114015812">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph0959144013587">Global Memory</span>. If the address value is equal to the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b775317216330">compare</strong>, the address value is set to the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6754142116339">val</strong>. Otherwise, the address value remains unchanged.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1819812308522">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18837203213524"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10383.html">asc_atomic_and</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p919417304529">Performs an atomic AND (&amp;) operation on the address value in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph18404486584">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph138406481583">Global Memory</span> and the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b8563143615337">val</strong>, assigning the result to the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph7927849219">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4927742218">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11982030105210">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1837163211522"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10384.html">asc_atomic_or</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15194130125218">Performs an atomic OR (|) operation on the address value in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph652716542215">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1652785412220">Global Memory</span> and the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b782319063420">val</strong>, assigning the result to the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1333582155916">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph13335112125912">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row819816309523">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p108358323529"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10385.html">asc_atomic_xor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8194203065216">Performs an atomic XOR (^) operation on the address value in the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph12461111115918">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph19461181110597">Global Memory</span> and the specified value <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b55751263341">val</strong>, assigning the result to the <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph746121145910">Unified Buffer</span> or <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1946111113591">Global Memory</span>.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table13746514532" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 33 </b>Warp functions
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row53744575316">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p133744575314">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1137419545315">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row63742535318">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p137411525310"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10387.html">asc_all</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p118785325243">Checks whether the inputs of all active threads are not 0.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1374145125316">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3374165145317"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10388.html">asc_any</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p361320505593">Checks whether the input of any active thread is not 0.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row937495135320">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1237412595310"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10389.html">asc_ballot</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p178511254143017">Checks whether the input of each active thread in a warp is not 0.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row937475185312">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18374152538"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10390.html">asc_activemask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13965195011409">Checks whether all threads in a warp are active.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16374452533">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p737413565311"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10391.html">asc_shfl</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8374185115315">Obtains the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b025410123713">var</strong> value input by the specified thread srcLane in a warp for swapping.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13747585320">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p337419535319"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10392.html">asc_shfl_up</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1337411519534">Obtains the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b20486623123818">var</strong> value input by the thread that is delta lanes forward from the current thread in the warp (current LaneId – delta) for swapping.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row193742515530">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p637420511533"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10393.html">asc_shfl_down</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p537455125315">Obtains the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b9956115810384">var</strong> value input by the thread that is delta lanes backward from the current thread in a warp (current LaneId + delta) for swapping.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row103591959165310">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19356125955319"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10394.html">asc_shfl_xor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14356125916533">Obtains the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b108031645394">var</strong> value input by the thread corresponding to <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b68031747398">dstLaneId</strong> (obtained by performing an XOR operation on the current LaneId and the input laneMask: LaneId^laneMask) for swapping.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row335915918538">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p163562059105319"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10395.html">asc_reduce_add</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6356195985310">Sums up the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b19198952124219">val</strong> values input by all active threads in a warp.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18358459195316">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p183561590539"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10396.html">asc_reduce_max</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3356759135312">Computes the maximum value of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b87632412435">val</strong> input by all active threads in a warp.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6358459175313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p83567590530"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10397.html">asc_reduce_min</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1235655945315">Computes the minimum value of <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b11347101118435">val</strong> input by all active threads in a warp.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table113998363475" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 34 </b>Type conversion
      </caption>
