    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table17209142275014" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>Table 36 </b>Load/Store functions with cache hints enabled
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row120922211507">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1220962210501">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p620912295018">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row180373015523">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p68031330105213"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10556.html">asc_ldcg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2094931913815">Loads cached data from the L2 cache. If the cache hit occurs, the data is returned directly. If the cache miss occurs, the data is preloaded from <span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4433133645514">Global Memory</span> into L2 Cache and then returned.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row185241328175218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1352432816526"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10557.html">asc_ldca</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p95231912151116">First loads cached data from data cache. If cache miss occurs, it attempts to load from L2 cache. If the required data is not found in either data cache or L2 cache, it is fetched from global memory and then cached into both L2 cache and data cache.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row83103233528">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13310182315219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10558.html">asc_stcg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10735143121114">Stores specified data to the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1576341611712">address</strong> of the global memory and caches the data to the L2 cache, but not to the data cache.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row142096228500">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2209172255013"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10559.html">asc_stwt</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19524191351212">Stores specified data to the <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b109281519181711">address</strong> of the global memory and caches the data to the data cache and L2 cache.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>

      <caption>
       <b>Table 38 </b>APIs for obtaining platform information
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13299184712616">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.6%" id="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15299104762613">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.4%" id="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.2">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row63002047192611">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.6%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p530034762613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1026.html">PlatformAscendC</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.4%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p43001947152612">Obtains hardware platform information, such as the number of cores on a hardware platform for tiling computation to implement the tiling function on the host. The PlatformAscendC class provides a function for obtaining such platform information.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6300134719269">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.6%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14300184720265"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1039.html">PlatformAscendCManager</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.4%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1330019473269">Obtains hardware platform information, such as the number of cores on the hardware platform, to call operators in the basic mode (kernel launch) based on the kernel launch operator project. The PlatformAscendCManager class provides the function of obtaining platform information.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1294106171920"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0978.html">OpAICoreDef</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1594116181913">Defines the implementation information of the AI Processor and associates the tiling implementation and shape inference functions.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2949661912">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1194166101912"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0988.html">OpAICoreConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19947620190">Configures AI Core information.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10946641913">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1994186111919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0999.html">OpMC2Def</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p69420601919">Configures the communication domain name of an MC2 operator on the host. Once the domain is configured, the kernel side can retrieve the corresponding context address associated with that communication domain.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

      </caption>

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" id="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.2">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1319034531918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10190245111913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1005.html">TilingData Structure Definition</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p619064571919">Defines a TilingData class and adds required member variables (TilingData fields) to store required TilingData parameters. After the TilingData class is defined, this class inherits the TilingDef class (base class for storing and processing user-defined Tiling structure member variables) to provide APIs for setting, serializing, and saving TilingData fields.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row619054501914">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1219044520192"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1006.html">TilingData Structure Registration</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1019034511196">Registers the defined TilingData structure and binds it with a custom operator.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">

      </caption>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2010212532219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00071.html">OpTilingRegistry</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p710211522210"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph134802046173619">The OpTilingRegistry class belongs to the context_ascendc namespace. It is mainly used to load the dynamic library for tiling implementation and obtain the tiling function pointer of the operator for debugging and verification. </span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1867785432614">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p967795492618"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1007.html">ContextBuilder</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p56781254122619">Provides a series of APIs for you to manually build the <a href="/document/detail/en/CANNCommunityEdition/900/maintenref/basicdataapi/atlasopapi_07_00223.html">TilingContext</a> class to verify the tiling functions and the KernelContext class to verify the TilingParse functions.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" id="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1486515413204">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p111901452192"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00011.html">Template Argument Definition</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5191124551911">Defines the template argument declaration <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1435316194440">ASCENDC_TPL_ARGS_DECL</strong> and the template argument combination <strong id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b6353161904415">ASCENDC_TPL_ARGS_SEL</strong> (available template).</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row198667417207">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17191145131910"><a href="atlas_ascendc_10_00008.html">GET_TPL_TILING_KEY</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1619174520193">Automatically generates a TilingKey during tiling template programming. This API converts the passed template arguments into binary values based on the defined bit width, combines the binary values in sequence, and then converts the values into uint64, that is, TilingKey.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row586654192019">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p419110458198"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00057.html">ASCENDC_TPL_SEL_PARAM</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p819119458193"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1519110455190">Automatically generates and configures a TilingKey during tiling template programming.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.3%" headers="mcps1.3.8.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1419164561913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00060.html">DEVICE_IMPL_OP_OPTILING</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.7%" headers="mcps1.3.8.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9191945151920"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph419114452199">Generates a registration class for tiling offload in the <a href="/document/detail/en/CANNCommunityEdition/900/programug/Ascendcopdevg/atlas_ascendc_10_00014.html">tiling offload</a> scenario, and calls member functions of the registration class to register the tiling functions to be offloaded.</span></p></td>

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149052056172612"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph17905125672617">Compiles a specified program.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3905185632610">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99051356182611"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00155.html">aclrtcCreateProg</a></p></td>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1490685613261"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph169061356152611">Destroys an instance of the compiler.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8906956122613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2906135614269"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph190617563264">Obtains the size of the compiled binary data. This API is used to allocate memory space when the <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00157.html">aclrtcGetBinData</a> API is used to obtain the binary data.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row29061056112617">

         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19906165612617"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1290635618262">Obtains the size of the compilation log. This API is used to allocate the memory space when the <a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00160.html">aclrtcGetCompileLog</a> API is used to obtain the log content.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row9907185610267">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16907105652615"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00160.html">aclrtcGetCompileLog</a></p></td>

       </tr>

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row914623311278">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p014643382720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00152.html">ASC_CPU_LOG</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101461833162715"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1014643313273">Provides the function of printing logs on the host. You can use the ASC_CPU_LOG_XXX API in the TilingFunc code of the operator to output related content.</span></p></td>
       </tr>

    <div class="tablenoborder">

      <caption>
       <b>Table 46 </b>Debugging APIs
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10826162551319">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" id="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p782622512134">API</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" id="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p982712512136">Function</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row48271425151314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p128276255138"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10426.html">printf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p182718253134"><span id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1565753361414">In the operator kernel implementation code, call the printf API to print related content when log information needs to be output.</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1597951912417">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p397971912416"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10428.html">assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p697901913247">Provides the assert function in SIMT VF debugging scenarios. In the SIMT VF implementation code on the operator kernel side, if the internal assert condition evaluates to false, the condition is output and the input information is formatted and printed to the screen.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row497811224246">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19781122132416"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10429.html">__trap</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1297852218244">Calling this API in the SIMT VF implementation code will interrupt the operator execution.</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1765213536149">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19652105310140"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10448.html">clock</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="EN-US_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p277505112411">Provides the clock timestamp function in the SIMT VF debugging scenario. It records the number of clock cycles (Cycle Count) from program start to the moment of API invocation, enabling precise analysis of execution latency and performance bottlenecks.</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>
